We can implement an iterative projected gradient on simplex:
- Normalize a to sum1.
- Repeat until time:
  - Compute c=conv(a,a) via FFT length L=nextpow2(2n-1), take first 2n-1.
  - Identify active set: c_k > threshold (e.g., max - tol). Choose weights w_k proportional exp(β(c_k - max)) or binary top k.
  - Compute grad_j=2Σ_{active} w_k a_{k-j} (for valid k-j). This can be computed by convolving w (length 2n-1) with a? Specifically grad_j = 2 Σ_{k} w_k a_{k-j} (where valid). The LP step in SOTA fixed f and M, maximizing sum g subject f*g≤M. That is a linearization of the upper level: for current f, constraints linear in g. Then update f. We can implement this LP approximately using subgradient/projected: For fixed f, maximize sum g subject T_f g ≤ M. Since f fixed nonnegative, T_f nonnegative. The optimum g is supported on indices where many constraints inactive. We can use multiplicative weights for constraints: Initialize g=f; iterate g_i ∝ g_i exp(ν (1 - (T_f g)_i/M))? This performs logarithmic barrier for constraints; at convergence, KKT same as dual. This is a Frank-Wolfe-like method for LP. Could be faster than solving LP and captures the SOTA step. Then blend f. Let's explore.

Step update:
- We maintain a normalized to sum 1 (or unnormalized? Let's normalize to 1 for update). a = a / S.
- c, active, grad.
- Weighted mean mu = dot(a,grad).
- g = grad - mu.
- Use scale: sigma = max(abs(g)); if sigma==0, break.
- Baseline eta = step_param / sigma. step_param maybe 0.5? For normalized a, derivative of c~1/n, sigma~a~1/√n=0.015, eta~33. exp changes moderate. But c~1/n=0.00024; objective change due to step ~eta*g*c? Could be. Use backtracking factors [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,...]. If no improvement, accept factor=0.01 maybe? But many no improvements could stagnate.

But the direction -g may not decrease max if active set changes. Use line search to find best among factors. If best factor <min, we can take a random small perturbation instead.

```

Function eval_for_opt(coeffs, n, base_B, beta):

Mirror pseudocode:
```
def mirror_improve(a, max_time, seed):
    n=len(a); a=a.copy(); a=a/a.sum(); best=a.copy(); best_score=score_fast(a)
    deadline=time+max_time
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    stall=0
    L=nextpow2(3*n-1)
    iter=0
    while time<deadline:
        S=a.sum(); a=a/S
        fa=irfft? rfft(a,L)
        c=irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
        maxc=c.max()
        beta=10.0/maxc if maxc>0 else 0
        u=beta*(c-maxc)
        u=np.clip(u,-50,0)
        p=np.exp(u); p/=p.sum()
        fp=rfft(p,L)
        h=irfft(fp*np.conj(fa), L)[:n]
        grad=2*h
        grad=np.maximum? no negative? Should nonnegative but numerical.
        mu=np.dot(a,grad)/S
        g=grad-mu
        mg=np.max(np.abs(g))
        if mg < 1e-12: random...
        base_eta = 1.0/mg
        # actual step uses a normalized, so include scale? a*exp(-mult*base_eta*g)
        best_mult=None
        for mult in [0.5,0.25,0.1,1.0,2.0,4.0]:? choose order? 
            cand=a*np.exp(-mult*base_eta*g) # but g centered weighted, exponent mean zero approx
            # if many underflow, cand sum may small; normalize; if sum<1e-12, skip
            cand=np.clip(cand,0,1e100); cs=cand.sum(); if cs==0: continue
            cand=cs/cand? cand=cand/cs
            sc=score_fast(cand)
            if sc<best_score:
                best_mult=mult; best_a_cand=cand; best_score=sc
        if accepted: a=cand; stall=0; iter++
        else:
            # try a random block perturbation around a
            cand=block_pert(a,rng, strength maybe 0.05)
            sc=score
            if sc<best_score: accept
            else: stall++
            if stall>10: maybe lower active focus / random restart partial
            stall++
```

def lp_alternate(f, M, n, tol):
   try import scipy.optimize as opt, scipy.sparse as sp
   Build A (2n-1,n) sparse: for i in range(n): rows = i:i+n, cols i, values f? Need A_scipy with A[i?]. Primal: A_ub @ g <= M.
   A_ub = sp.coo_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(2n-1,n)).tocsr()
   c = -np.ones(n)  # minimize -sum
   res=opt.linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b=np.full(2n-1,M), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve':'highs'} )
   if res.success: return res.x
```

def lp_optimize(a, iters, time_limit, seed):
    n=a.size; a=a/a.sum(); best=a; best_score=score_fast(a)
    for it in range(iters):
       if time exceeded break
       c=conv_fast(a); M=c.max()
       g=lp_best_response(a,M,n)
       if g is None: break
       sg=g.sum(); if sg<=0: break
       g=g/sg
       for t in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5]:
           cand=(1-t)*a+t*g
           cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000); cand=cand/cand.sum()
           sc=score_fast(cand)
           if sc<best_score: accept, break
       if no accept, smaller t or break after stall
    return best

def resample_seq(a, m):
    n=len(a)
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    # linear interpolation on centers? positions old i, new i = j*(n-1)/(m-1)
    pos=np.linspace(0,m-1,m)*(n-1)/(m-1)
    return np.interp(pos, np.arange(n), a)
```

def random_perturb(a,rng, strength=0.1):
    cand=a.copy()
    # block exp normal
    width = max(2, int(rng.integers(n//200, n//20)))
    start = rng.integers(0, n-width)
    cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, strength))
    if rng.random()<0.3: cand = 0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])? Symmetrize may move away; maybe less.
    cand=np.clip(cand,0,1e6); cand/=cand.sum()
    return cand
```

Function structure:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

def _lb_eval(coeffs):

1. `next_pow2(m)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```

Use `L = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()` for m>1. If m=2, (1).bit_length=1 ->2.

2. `make_binomial(n)`:
```
def make_binomial(n):
    b=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64); b[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
       b[i]=b[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2.0*i)
    return b
```
For n huge, loop Python 32768 okay. Could vectorize cumulative product:
```
i=np.arange(1,n)
ratios=(2*i-1)/(2*i)
b[1:]=np.exp(np.cumsum(np.log(ratios)))
```
Loop fine for 32768. For 100k maybe loop slower but okay. Use vectorized for speed:
```
i = np.arange(1,n,dtype=np.float64)
b[1:] = (2*i-1)/(2*i) ; b[1:] = np.cumprod(b[1:])
```
But building (2i-1) each. Simpler loop.

3. `convolution_full(a, L=None)`:
```
def convolution_full(a, L=None):
    n=len(a)
    if L is None:
       L=next_pow2(max(2, 2*n-1))
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
4. `score_fast(a)`:
```
def score_fast(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a); S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-300 or not finite: return inf
    c=convolution_full(a)
    maxc=c.max()
    if maxc<0: return inf
    return float(2*n*maxc/(S*S))
```
For mirror, we often have c; use internal.

5. `cross_correlation_p(p, a, L)`:
```
def cross_correlation_p(p, a, L):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(fp*np.conj(fa), L)[:len(a)]
    return np.maximum(h,0.0)
```
In mirror, we compute fa once and pass? Make helper `mirror_step_components(a,L)` returning c, fa. To avoid recompute fa for h? We can in mirror code compute manually.

6. `random_block_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.15)`:
```
def random_block_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.15):
    n=len(a); cand=a.copy()
    width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//20)))
    start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n-width+1)))
    factor=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength))
    cand[start:start+width]*=factor
    if rng.random()<0.2:
       # global scale? no
    cand=np.clip(cand,0.0,1e100)
    s=cand.sum()
    if s<=0: return a.copy()
    cand/=s
    return cand
```
Maybe also if n large use multiple blocks.

```
def mirror_search(a, time_budget, seed):
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); S=a.sum(); if S<=0...; a/=S
    n=a.size
    L=next_pow2(max(2, 3*n-1))
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0,time_budget)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_fast(a)
    stall=0; iter=0
    logit_last=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       iter+=1
       # current normalized
       S=a.sum(); a=a/S
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
       maxc=c.max()
       if not finite or maxc<=0: break
       beta=10.0/maxc
       u=beta*(c-maxc); u=np.clip(u,-50,0)
       p=np.exp(u); ps=p.sum(); if ps<=0: p=c/maxc? ; p/=ps
       fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
       h=np.fft.irfft(fp*np.conj(fa), L)[:n]; h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
       grad=2.0*h
       # numerical clip grad finite
       if not finite: random...
       mu=(a*grad).sum()  # since sum a=1? S=1
       g=grad - mu
       mg=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if mg <= 1e-14:
           # random step
           cand=random_block_perturb(a,rng,0.2)
           sc=score_fast(cand)
           if sc<best_score: best=cand; best_score=sc
           # do not move? maybe set current=cand if not too worse
           a=random_block_perturb(a,rng,0.2); break? 
           stall+=1
       else:
           base_eta=1.0/mg
           accepted=False
           for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,2.0,4.0):
              z=-mult*base_eta*g
              z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
              cand=a*np.exp(z)
              cand=np.clip(cand,0.0,1e100)
              cs=cand.sum()
              if cs<=0: continue
              cand/=cs
              sc=score_fast(cand)
              if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                  a=cand; best=cand.copy(); best_score=sc; accepted=True; break
           if not accepted:
              # try a very small step or random if stall
              if stall>20:
                  a=random_block_perturb(a,rng,0.3)
              else:
                  # take downhill fixed? maybe no
                  stall+=1
       if iter % 100 ==0: print...
       if stall > 200:
           # restart current from binomial with noise or best
           a= random_block_perturb(best, rng, 0.5)
           # or binomial_noisy:
           # a=make_binomial(n) * exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n)); normalize
           stall=0
    return best, best_score
```
Need avoid infinite loop if no accepted; current stays same, stall grows, then random. Good.

Line search choosing order: If mult=1 always improves maybe accept; if not try smaller. But if mult=1 not improve but mult=2 improves? Rare. Try [1,0.5,2,0.25,4,0.1]? To save, [1,0.5,0.25,2,0.1]. Score evaluations up to5. Could be costly. We can first evaluate only mult=1 then 0.5 then 2. Let's use (1.0,0.5,2.0,0.25,4.0,0.1) but break.

Maybe step 1 is too large; if no improve, smaller likely improve. Good.

To reduce score evaluations, we can compute candidate score via `score_fast(cand)` which uses c length 4n? Actually score L next pow2 2n. Mirror main L=3n, but score uses L 2n. Fine.

`stall > 200` maybe too many. Use 100.

8. `lowdim_run(n, time_budget, seed, best_seq_holder?)`
Return best a and score.
Parameters P small/large. We can run nested:
```
def lowdim_run(n, budget, seed):
    B=make_binomial(n)
    t_build...
    basis = ... P up to 12
    best_b=B normalized? Keep unnormalized? best_seq = B.copy(); best_score=score_fast(B)
    # sets for P=5,8,12; starts per P
    global _LB_BASE,_LB_BASIS,_LB_BETA,_LB_L,_LB_CACHE,_LB_OFFSET
    for P in [5,8,12]: if time left
       basis_P = b[:P]
       set globals
       for start in [zero, random1, random2]:
          x0 = np.zeros(P); or rng.normal(0,0.7,P)
          reset cache/offset
          try res=scipy_opt.minimize(_lb_eval, x0, jac=_lb_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(-8,8)]*P, options={...})
          x=res.x; a=B*exp(basis_P@x); sc=score_fast(a); update
          # also try simple random coordinate search around x for e.g. 2000 evals? maybe.
```
The `basis` for low-dim can include log column and powers. If P changes, basis nested.
Basis construction:
```
def make_basis(n,P):
   t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   x=2*t-1
   cols=[]
   cols.append(np.log(np.arange(1,n+1)))  # length n; log(i+1)
   for m in range(1,P):
       # alternate powers? use x**m
       cols.append(x**m)
   return np.column_stack(cols)

Implement:
```
def lp_best_response(f, M):
    n=f.size
    rows=[]; cols=[]; vals=[]
    scale=1.0/M
    f_scaled=f*scale
    for i in range(n):
       rows.extend(range(i,i+n))
       cols.extend([i]*n)
       vals.extend(f_scaled)
    A=sp.coo_matrix((vals,(rows,cols)),shape=(2*n-1,n)).tocsr()
    c=-np.ones(n)
    res=opt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b=np.ones(2*n-1), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':'highs'})
    if res.success: return res.x
    return None
```
Memory: `vals` list of n lists length n; for n=1024 okay but Python lists overhead. Better preallocate arrays? Use `rows = np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)`? For column i, rows i..i+n-1. Could build with arrays:
```
nlen = 2*n-1
cols = np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)
rows = np.tile(np.arange(n), n) + np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)  # i + j
vals = np.tile(f_scaled, n)
A=sp.coo_matrix((vals.astype(np.float64),(rows,cols)),shape=(nlen,n))
```
Where `j` from0..n-1; row=i+j. `np.repeat(arange(n), n)` gives cols; `np.tile(arange(n), n)` gives j; add. vals tile f_scaled. This is O(n^2) arrays in C. For n=1024, good. For n=2048, 4M arrays ~32MB each, 3 arrays 96MB; okay but maybe. Limit n_lp=1024.

Implementation of `_lb_objective_and_grad`:
```
def _lb_objective_and_grad(coeffs):
    base = _LB_BASE
    basis = _LB_BASIS
    beta = _LB_BETA
    L = _LB_L
    n = base.size
    p = basis.dot(coeffs)
    p = np.clip(p, -50, 50)
    a = base * np.exp(p)
    S = a.sum()
    if S <= 0 or not np.isfinite(S):
       # return large val and zeros grad
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0)
    maxc = c.max()
    if maxc <= 0 or not finite: ...
    u = beta*(c - maxc)
    u = np.clip(u, -50, 0)
    e = np.exp(u)
    e_sum = e.sum()
    if e_sum <=0: e=np.ones_like(c); e_sum=c.size
    w = e / e_sum
    logsum_beta = beta*maxc + np.log(e_sum)
    val = logsum_beta - beta*np.log(2*n-1) - 2.0*beta*np.log(S)
    # gradient
    fp = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conj(fa), L)[:n]
    h = np.maximum(h, 0)
    ah = a*h
    aa = a*a
    term1 = 2.0 * (ah @ basis.T) # basis shape (n,P)
    term2 = (2.0 / S) * (aa @ basis.T)
    grad = beta * (term1 - term2)
    # if grad has nonfinite, zero
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=np.zeros_like(coeffs)
    if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e300
    return float(val), grad
```
But `basis @ coeffs` where basis 2D. `ah @ basis.T` okay. Could use `basis.T @ ah` for clarity. Precompute `basis` as C-contiguous 2D.

Potential issue: If β large (e.g., 100) and maxc large, `beta*maxc` huge but okay. However L-BFGS may overflow if val ~1e6; not. Use offset.

Beta schedule for lowdim: Start β=50, then 150, maybe 400. Since c max binomial ~1; β=150 focuses. For high-dim mirror beta=10/maxc independent.

The objective `val` is not exactly score; random coefficient hill climb should use true score, not val. For L-BFGS, val okay.

`_lb_eval` wrapper:
```
def _lb_eval(coeffs):
    global _LB_CACHE, _LB_OFFSET
    if _LB_CACHE is not None:
       c0,v,g = _LB_CACHE
       if c0.shape == coeffs.shape and np.array_equal(c0, coeffs):
           return v
    v_raw,g = _lb_objective_and_grad(np.asarray(coeffs,dtype=np.float64))
    if _LB_OFFSET is None:
       _LB_OFFSET = v_raw if np.isfinite(v_raw) else 0.0
    v = v_raw - _LB_OFFSET
    if not np.isfinite(v): v=1e300
    _LB_CACHE = (np.asarray(coeffs,dtype=np.float64).copy(), v, g)
    return v
def _lb_grad(coeffs):
    similar, if cache hit return g; else compute, offset not needed for grad, but store.
```
If L-BFGS calls grad at a new point and offset None (unlikely), set offset.

Random coefficient hill climb:
```
def coeff_hill_climb(x, basis, base, rng, max_iters, deadline):
    n=base.size
    def eval_x(x):
       a=base*np.exp(np.clip(basis@x,-50,50)); return score_fast(a), a
    x=x.copy(); sc,a=eval_x(x); best=x.copy(); best_sc=sc
    stall=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       y=x.copy()
       # random subset
       k=int(min(len(y), rng.integers(1, max(2,len(y)//2+1))))
       idx=rng.choice(len(y), k, replace=False)
       y[idx]+=rng.normal(0,0.4,k)
       y=np.clip(y,-8,8)
       sc2,a2=eval_x(y)
       if sc2<sc: x=y; sc=sc2; best=x.copy(); best_sc=sc2; stall=0
       else:
          stall+=1
          if stall>20:
             x = best + rng.normal(0, 0.8, len(x))
             x = np.clip(x,-8,8)
             sc = ...
             stall=0
    return best,best_sc
```

Simulated annealing polish:
```
def anneal_polish(a, time_budget, seed):
    current=normalize; best=current; cur=score; best=...
    n=len; T=0.1? Since score range ~1.5, T=0.05.
    iters=0
    while time:
       cand=block_perturb(current)
       sc=score(cand)
       if sc<cur or rng.random()<np.exp(-(sc-cur)/T): current=cand; cur=sc
       if sc<best_score: best=cand
       T*=0.9995
       iters++
    return best
```

   b=raw/S
   L=...; fa=rfft(b,L); c=irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2n-1]; maxc=c.max()
   u=beta*(c-maxc); w=exp(u); w/=sum
   val = beta*maxc + log(sumexp) - beta*logK   # or shifted? maxc shape-dependent; keep.
   fp=rfft(w,L); h=irfft(fp*conj(fa),L)[:n]
   gb = beta*2*h   # derivative wrt b
   # grad per coeff
   # term_sum = Σ_j b_j gb_j q_m(j) for each m
   temp = (b*gb) @ basis.T  # length P: Qgb_m

Anneal code:
```
def anneal_polish(a, time_budget, seed):
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); a/=a.sum()
    n=a.size; best=a; best_score=score_fast(a); current=a; cur_score=best_score
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_budget
    T=0.05
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        cand=random_block_perturb(current, rng, 0.25)
        sc=score_fast(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < cur_score or rng.random() < np.exp(-(sc-cur_score)/T):
                current=cand; cur_score=sc
            if sc < best_score: best=cand; best_score=sc
        it+=1; T*=0.999; if T<1e-6: T=1e-6
        if it%2000==0: print...
    return best,best_score
```
If current becomes far from best due to worse accepts, random restarts? Add if cur_score > best_score +0.2: current=best.copy() reset. Good.

def resample_to_length(a, m):
   n=a.size; if n==1: return np.full(m,a.sum())
   old=np.arange(n); new=np.linspace(0,n-1,m); vals=np.interp(new,old,a); vals=np.clip(vals,0,1e100); vals+=1e-18; vals/=vals.sum(); return vals

```
def make_basis(n,P):
    # first column log; remaining P-1 cos
    if P<=0: return np.empty((n,0))
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    cols=[np.log(np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64))]
    for m in range(1,P): cols.append(np.cos(np.pi*m*t))
    mat=np.column_stack(cols)
    return mat
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative coefficient sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a)**2).

Main ingredients:
  * binomial prefix seeds, which are strong one-dimensional initializers;
  * low-dimensional smooth coefficient optimization on a basis
    (log tail + cosine modes) using a stable log-sum-exp surrogate;
  * high-dimensional mirror-descent/random-restart refinement;
  * an optional small-size LP alternating response step inspired by the
    previous state of the art;
  * final simulated-annealing-like block polishing.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy import optimize as scipy_opt
    from scipy import sparse as scipy_sp
except Exception:
    scipy_opt = None
    scipy_sp = None

# Globals used by the top-level low-dimensional objective functions.
_LB_BASE = None
_LB_BASIS = None
_LB_BETA = 10.0
_LB_L = 2
_LB_CACHE = None
_LB_OFFSET = None

_FALLBACK_SEQ = None


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(2, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def make_binomial(n):
    """Binomial coefficients C(2k,k)/4^k for k = 0..n-1."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    b = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        b[i] = b[i - 1] * (2 * i - 1) / (2.0 * i)
    return b


def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0e100)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1.0e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.shape, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def convolution_full(a, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return c


def score_fast(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0e100)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1.0e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    c = convolution_full(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def random_block_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.25):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 1:
        return normalize_nonnegative(a)

    cand = a.copy()
    c = cand.sum()
    if c <= 0:
        return normalize_nonnegative(cand)
    cand /= c

    lo = max(1, min(n, n // 200))
    hi = max(lo, min(n, max(2, n // 20)))
    if hi > n:
        hi = n
    width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
    stop = min(n, start + width)

    if width > 0:
        cand[start:stop] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength)))
        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            # Occasionally mix in a mirror version to break endpoint bias.
            mirror = cand[::-1].copy()
            cand = 0.75 * cand + 0.25 * mirror

    cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1.0e100)
    cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
    s = cand.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        return normalize_nonnegative(a)
    cand /= s
    cand += 1.0e-18
    cand /= cand.sum()
    return cand


def make_basis(n, P):
    """
    Low-dimensional log-multipliers:
       col 0: log(i+1), to change effective endpoint singular exponent
       col m: cos(m*pi*t), smooth global shape features
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    P = int(max(1, P))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    cols = [np.log(np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64))]
    for m in range(1, P):
        cols.append(np.cos(np.pi * m * t))
    return np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64)


def _lb_compute(coeffs):
    global _LB_BASE, _LB_BASIS, _LB_BETA, _LB_L, _LB_CACHE, _LB_OFFSET

    if _LB_BASE is None or _LB_BASIS is None:
        return float("inf"), None

    coeffs = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    base = _LB_BASE
    basis = _LB_BASIS
    beta = float(_LB_BETA)
    L = int(_LB_L)
    n = base.size

    p = basis.dot(coeffs)
    p = np.clip(p, -8.0, 8.0)
    raw = base * np.exp(p)
    s = float(raw.sum())
    if s <= 1.0e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1.0e300, np.zeros_like(coeffs)

    b = raw / s
    fa = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    if maxc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return 1.0e300, np.zeros_like(coeffs)

    u = beta * (c - maxc)
    u = np.clip(u, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(u)
    e_sum = float(e.sum())
    if e_sum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(e_sum):
        e = np.ones_like(c)
        e_sum = float(e.size)
    w = e / e_sum

    val = beta * maxc + np.log(e_sum)

    # Exact derivative of logsumexp(beta*c(b)) on the simplex, mapped to coeffs.
    fp = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conj(fa), L)[:n]
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)

    gb = beta * (2.0 * h)
    temp = (b * gb) @ basis.T
    qm = b @ basis.T
    total = float(np.dot(b, gb))
    grad = temp - total * qm

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros_like(coeffs)

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1.0e300

    return float(val), grad


def _lb_eval(coeffs):
    global _LB_CACHE, _LB_OFFSET

    coeffs_arr = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if _LB_CACHE is not None:
        c0, v0, g0 = _LB_CACHE
        if c0.shape == coeffs_arr.shape and np.array_equal(c0, coeffs_arr):
            return v0

    v_raw, g_raw = _lb_compute(coeffs_arr)
    if _LB_OFFSET is None:
        if np.isfinite(v_raw):
            _LB_OFFSET = float(v_raw)
        else:
            _LB_OFFSET = 0.0
    v_out = float(v_raw - _LB_OFFSET) if np.isfinite(v_raw) else 1.0e300
    _LB_CACHE = (coeffs_arr.copy(), v_out, g_raw)
    return v_out


def _lb_grad(coeffs):
    global _LB_CACHE, _LB_OFFSET

    coeffs_arr = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if _LB_CACHE is not None:
        c0, v0, g0 = _LB_CACHE
        if c0.shape == coeffs_arr.shape and np.array_equal(c0, coeffs_arr):
            return g0

    v_raw, g_raw = _lb_compute(coeffs_arr)
    if _LB_OFFSET is None:
        if np.isfinite(v_raw):
            _LB_OFFSET = float(v_raw)
        else:
            _LB_OFFSET = 0.0
    v_out = float(v_raw - _LB_OFFSET) if np.isfinite(v_raw) else 1.0e300
    if g_raw is None:
        g_raw = np.zeros(coeffs_arr.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    _LB_CACHE = (coeffs_arr.copy(), v_out, g_raw)
    return g_raw


def make_seq_from_coeffs(coeffs, basis, base):
    coeffs = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = basis.dot(coeffs)
    p = np.clip(p, -8.0, 8.0)
    a = base * np.exp(p)
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def lp_best_response(f, M):
    """
    Solve the LP from the SOTA idea:
      maximize sum(g)  subject to  (f*g)_k <= M, g>=0.
    Return 0 if no valid response is found.
    """
    if scipy_opt is None or scipy_sp is None:
        return None
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    n = f.size
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if M <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    f_scaled = f / M
    cols = np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64), n)
    rows = np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64), n) + cols
    vals = np.tile(f_scaled.astype(np.float64), n)

    A = scipy_sp.coo_matrix(
        (vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n)
    ).tocsr()

    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    try:
        res = scipy_opt.linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b=b,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": "highs", "time_limit": 20.0},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = scipy_opt.linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b=b,
                bounds=bounds,
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

    if not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None

    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    if x.sum() <= 1.0e-300:
        return None
    return x


def lp_optimize(a, deadline, max_iters=35):
    """Alternating LP response updates on a normalized sequence."""
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_fast(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        c = convolution_full(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        g = lp_best_response(a, M)
        if g is None:
            break
        g = normalize_nonnegative(g)

        accepted = False
        for t in (0.10, 0.04, 0.20, 0.02, 0.35, 0.60, 0.50):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
            cand = normalize_nonnegative(cand)
            sc = score_fast(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1.0e-12:
                a = cand
                best = cand.copy()
                best_score = sc
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            # If the LP direction helps even slightly, take a tiny step.
            cand = 0.98 * a + 0.02 * g
            cand = normalize_nonnegative(cand)
            sc = score_fast(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1.0e-12:
                a = cand
                best = cand.copy()
                best_score = sc
            else:
                # Plateau: perturb the current point a little and try once more.
                a = normalize_nonnegative(a + 0.02 * (g - a))
                c2 = convolution_full(a)
                M2 = float(c2.max())
                if M2 <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M2):
                    break
                g2 = lp_best_response(a, M2)
                if g2 is not None:
                    g2 = normalize_nonnegative(g2)
                    cand = 0.90 * a + 0.10 * g2
                    cand = normalize_nonnegative(cand)
                    sc = score_fast(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1.0e-12:
                        a = cand
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                    else:
                        break
                else:
                    break

    return best, best_score


def resample_to_length(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or m <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == m:
        return normalize_nonnegative(a)
    old = np.arange(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, a.size - 1, m, dtype=np.float64)
    vals = np.interp(new, old, a)
    vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 0.0)
    vals = np.maximum(vals, 0.0)
    vals += 1.0e-18
    vals /= vals.sum()
    return vals


def lowdim_run(n, deadline, seed):
    """Low-dimensional coefficient optimization over a smooth basis."""
    if n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    if scipy_opt is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    base = make_binomial(n)
    base_score = score_fast(base)
    best_seq = base.copy()
    best_score = base_score

    Pps = [4, 7, 10]
    for P in Pps:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        basis = make_basis(n, P)
        global _LB_BASE, _LB_BASIS, _LB_BETA, _LB_L, _LB_CACHE, _LB_OFFSET
        _LB_BASE = base
        _LB_BASIS = basis
        _LB_BETA = 10.0 * float(n)
        _LB_L = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

        # Starts: zero plus a couple random smooth perturbations.
        starts = [np.zeros(P, dtype=np.float64)]
        for _ in range(2):
            starts.append(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=P))

        for x0 in starts:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break

            x0 = np.clip(x0, -5.0, 5.0)
            _LB_CACHE = None
            _LB_OFFSET = None

            x_best = x0.copy()
            seq_best = make_seq_from_coeffs(x_best, basis, base)
            sc_best = score_fast(seq_best)
            if np.isfinite(sc_best) and sc_best < best_score:
                best_seq = seq_best.copy()
                best_score = sc_best

            try:
                res = scipy_opt.minimize(
                    _lb_eval,
                    x_best,
                    jac=_lb_grad,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=[(-6.0, 6.0)] * P,
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 90,
                        "ftol": 1.0e-12,
                        "gtol": 1.0e-6,
                        "maxls": 40,
                        "disp": False,
                    },
                )
                if hasattr(res, "x"):
                    x_best = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
                    x_best = np.clip(x_best, -6.0, 6.0)
                    seq = make_seq_from_coeffs(x_best, basis, base)
                    sc = score_fast(seq)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < sc_best:
                        x_best = x_best.copy()
                        seq_best = seq.copy()
                        sc_best = sc

            except Exception:
                pass

            if np.isfinite(sc_best) and sc_best < best_score:
                best_score = sc_best
                best_seq = seq_best.copy()

    _LB_BASE = None
    _LB_BASIS = None
    _LB_CACHE = None
    _LB_OFFSET = None

    return best_seq, best_score


def mirror_search(a, deadline, seed):
    """
    High-dimensional mirror descent on the simplex.
    Uses a log-sum-exp weighting of constraints and line searches on true score.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = len(np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)))
    if n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    current = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    best = current.copy()
    best_score = score_fast(current)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return None, float("inf")

    L = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    beta_weights = 10.0 * float(n)

    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        current = normalize_nonnegative(current)
        a_cur = current

        fa = np.fft.rfft(a_cur, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[: 2 * n - 1]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        maxc = float(c.max())
        if maxc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxc):
            break

        u = beta_weights * (c - maxc)
        u = np.clip(u, -50.0, 0.0)
        p = np.exp(u)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ps):
            p = c / maxc if maxc > 0 else np.ones_like(c)
            ps = float(p.sum())
        p = p / max(1.0, ps)

        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        h = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conj(fa), L)[:n]
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)

        grad = 2.0 * h
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            grad = np.zeros_like(a_cur)

        mu = float(np.dot(a_cur, grad))  # sum(a_cur) is 1
        g = grad - mu
        mg = float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if g.size else 0.0

        if mg <= 1.0e-15:
            # Direction is tiny: kick and try random local moves.
            cand = random_block_perturb(current, rng, 0.30)
            sc = score_fast(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = cand.copy()
                best_score = sc
            current = cand
            stall += 5
        else:
            base_eta = 1.0 / mg
            accepted = False
            for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25, 4.0, 0.1):
                z = -mult * base_eta * g
                z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
                cand = a_cur * np.exp(z)
                cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                s = float(cand.sum())
                if s <= 1.0e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                cand = cand / s
                sc = score_fast(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1.0e-13:
                    current = cand
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    stall = 0
                    accepted = True
                    break

            if not accepted:
                stall += 1
                # Occasionally take a very small mirror step instead of waiting.
                if stall % 3 == 0:
                    z = -0.02 / mg * g
                    z = np.clip(z, -12.0, 12.0)
                    cand = normalize_nonnegative(a_cur * np.exp(z))
                    sc = score_fast(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1.0e-13:
                        current = cand
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                        stall = 0

        # Restart current if stuck, while preserving the global best.
        if stall > 120:
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                base_mix = make_binomial(n)
                base_mix *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, n))
                current = normalize_nonnegative(0.7 * best + 0.3 * base_mix)
            else:
                current = normalize_nonnegative(0.8 * best + 0.2 * make_binomial(n))
            stall = 0

        if it % 1000 == 0:
            print(f"mirror it={it} best={best_score:.10f} n={n}", flush=True)

    return best, max(0.0, best_score)


def anneal_polish(a, deadline, seed):
    """Block-based simulated annealing to polish the current best."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    current = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    best = current.copy()
    best_score = score_fast(current)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    cur_score = best_score
    T = 0.04
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        cand = random_block_perturb(current, rng, 0.20 + 0.20 * T)
        sc = score_fast(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < cur_score or rng.random() < np.exp(-max(0.0, sc - cur_score) / max(1e-12, T)):
                current = cand
                cur_score = sc
            if sc < best_score:
                best = cand.copy()
                best_score = sc

        if cur_score > best_score + 0.25:
            current = best.copy()
            cur_score = best_score

        T *= 0.9995
        if T < 1.0e-6:
            T = 1.0e-6

        if it % 3000 == 0:
            print(f"polish it={it} best={best_score:.10f} current={cur_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point. Returns a Python list of non-negative heights.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0)
    deadline = t0 + budget

    def remaining():
        return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    seed = int(seed)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    def consider(seq, source=""):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        if seq is None:
            return
        seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
        if seq.size == 0:
            return
        seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
        seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
        s = float(seq.sum())
        if s < 1.0e-300:
            return
        seq = seq / s
        sc = score_fast(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1.0e-14:
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            best_score = sc
            print(f"new best [{source}] score={sc:.10f} n={seq.size}", flush=True)

    # Evaluate previous construction if it is present.
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        if hs is not None:
            consider(hs, "height_sequence_1")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Strong analytic seeds: binomial prefixes of moderate and large length.
    for n in (512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        b = make_binomial(n)
        consider(b, f"binomial-n{n}")

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = make_binomial(1024)
        best_score = score_fast(best_seq)

    # Working length for heavier optimization.
    n_work = 8192 if budget > 1500 else (4096 if budget > 600 else 2048)
    work_base = normalize_nonnegative(make_binomial(n_work))
    consider(work_base, f"work-base-n{n_work}")

    # 1) Optional small-scale LP alternating.
    have_lp = (
        scipy_opt is not None
        and scipy_sp is not None
        and budget > 1200
    )
    if have_lp:
        n_lp = 1024 if budget > 2200 else 512
        lp_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + min(300.0, 0.18 * budget))
        a_lp = normalize_nonnegative(make_binomial(n_lp))
        try:
            a_lp, sc_lp = lp_optimize(a_lp, lp_deadline, max_iters=35)
            if np.isfinite(sc_lp) and sc_lp < best_score - 1.0e-14:
                best_seq = normalize_nonnegative(a_lp).copy()
                best_score = sc_lp
                print(f"LP best score={sc_lp:.10f} n={n_lp}", flush=True)
            # Resample its shape to the working length.
            if time.monotonic() < deadline:
                a_res = resample_to_length(normalize_nonnegative(a_lp), n_work)
                sc_res = score_fast(a_res)
                print(f"LP resampled n={n_work} score={sc_res:.10f}", flush=True)
                if np.isfinite(sc_res) and sc_res < best_score - 1.0e-14:
                    best_seq = a_res.copy()
                    best_score = sc_res
        except Exception:
            pass

    # 2) Low-dimensional smooth optimization on working length.
    low_ddl = min(deadline, t0 + min(950.0, 0.45 * budget))
    lowdim_seq = None
    if low_ddl > time.monotonic():
        try:
            lowdim_seq, sc_low = lowdim_run(n_work, low_ddl, seed + 101)
            if lowdim_seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc_low):
                consider(lowdim_seq, "lowdim")
        except Exception:
            lowdim_seq = None

    # 3) High-dimensional mirror search from multiple starts.
    polish_reserve = min(250.0, 0.12 * budget) + 30.0
    mirror_ddl = max(time.monotonic() + 20.0, deadline - polish_reserve)
    starts = []

    st1 = normalize_nonnegative(make_binomial(n_work))
    sc1 = score_fast(st1)
    starts.append((sc1, st1, "mirror from binomial"))

    if lowdim_seq is not None and len(lowdim_seq) == n_work:
        st2 = normalize_nonnegative(lowdim_seq)
        sc2 = score_fast(st2)
        starts.append((sc2, st2, "mirror from lowdim"))

    # Maybe use the current best if it has exactly n_work length.
    if best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) == n_work:
        stb = normalize_nonnegative(best_seq)
        scb = score_fast(stb)
        for sc_, seq_, tag_ in starts:
            if np.isfinite(sc_) and np.array_equal(seq_, stb).all():
                scb = float("inf")
                break
        if np.sfinite if False else np.isfinite(scb):
            starts.append((scb, stb, "mirror from current best"))

    starts = [(sc, seq, tag) for (sc, seq, tag) in starts if np.isfinite(sc)]
    starts.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    starts = starts[:2]

    if starts and time.monotonic() < mirror_ddl:
        mirror_time = max(0.0, mirror_ddl - time.monotonic())
        if len(starts) == 1:
            time_weights = [1.0]
        else:
            time_weights = [0.65, 0.35]

        for i, (sc0, st0, tag0) in enumerate(starts):
            alloc = mirror_time * time_weights[i]
            if alloc < 15.0:
                break
            sub_deadline = min(mirror_ddl, time.monotonic() + alloc)
            try:
                a_out, sc_out = mirror_search(st0, sub_deadline, seed + 17 + 10 * i)
                if a_out is not None and np.isfinite(sc_out):
                    consider(a_out, tag0)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # 4) Final anneal polish around the best sequence found.
    if best_seq is not None and remaining() > 30.0:
        try:
            a_out, sc_out = anneal_polish(best_seq, deadline - 5.0, seed + 999)
            if a_out is not None and np.isfinite(sc_out):
                consider(a_out, "polish")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Fallback safety checks.
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = make_binomial(1024)
    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    best_seq = np.where(np.isfinite(best_seq), best_seq, 0.0)

    s = float(best_seq.sum())
    if s <= 1.0e-300:
        best_seq = normalize_nonnegative(make_binomial(1024))
        s = float(best_seq.sum())

    # If the search went somewhere pathological, fall back to binomial.
    if s < 0.01:
        best_seq = normalize_nonnegative(make_binomial(1024))
        s = float(best_seq.sum())

    # Return normalized sequence.
    best_seq = best_seq / s
    if best_seq.size == 0:
        best_seq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    # Optional sanity check with the official evaluator if available and cheap enough.
    try:
        if hasattr(eval, "evaluate_sequence") or True:
            if best_seq.size <= 8192 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
                off_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_seq]))
                if np.isfinite(off_score):
                    print(f"official sanity score={off_score:.10f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(
        f"FINAL_SCORE={best_score:.10f} length={best_seq.size} returns_in={max(0.0, deadline-time.monotonic()):.2f}s",
        flush=True,
    )
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
